The `public contributions` and `private contributions` by users in Oman on `2026/10/9 4:22 AM UTC`. This list contains users from `Oman` and cities `Muscat` `Salalah` `Nizwa` `Sohar` `Seeb`.

There are `138 countries` and `674 cities` can be found [here](https://github.com/gayanvoice/top-github-users).

There are `915 users`  in Oman. You need at least `1 followers` to be on this list.
